    def get_current_date_C1(self) -> None:
        """1300-get_current_date."""
        # ACCEPT ws_current_date FROM DATE YYYYMMDD
        # ACCEPT ws_current_time FROM TIME
        # STRING ws_current_date DELIMITED SIZE
        #         '-' DELIMITED SIZE
        #         ws_current_time DELIMITED SIZE
        #         INTO ws_current_timestamp
        now = datetime.datetime.now()
        self.WS_CURRENT_DATE = int(now.strftime("%Y%m%d"))
        self.WS_CURRENT_TIME = int(now.strftime("%H%M%S%f")[:8])
        self.WS_CURRENT_TIMESTAMP = now.strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S%f")[:26]

        if self.WS_CUST_STATUS != '00':
            print("ERROR: CUSTOMER FILE OPEN FAILED")
            self.WS_ERROR_FLAG = 'Y'  # SET ws_error TO TRUE
        if self.WS_ACCT_STATUS != '00':
            print("ERROR: ACCOUNT FILE OPEN FAILED")
            self.WS_ERROR_FLAG = 'Y'  # SET ws_error TO TRUE

    def process_banking_C1(self) -> None:
        """2000-process_banking."""
        self.process_deposits_C1()  # PERFORM 2100-process_deposits
        self.process_withdrawals_C1()  # PERFORM 2200-process_withdrawals
        self.process_transfers_C1()  # PERFORM 2300-process_transfers
        self.calculate_interest_C1()  # PERFORM 2400-calculate_interest
        self.apply_fees_C1()  # PERFORM 2500-apply_fees
        self.process_payments_C1()  # PERFORM 2600-process_payments
        self.reconcile_accounts_C1()  # PERFORM 2700-reconcile_accounts

    def process_deposits_C1(self) -> None

@dataclass
class CustomerMaster:

    cust_total_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

def calculate_premiums_C2() -> None:
    """4200-calculate_premiums"""
    DISPLAY "CALCULATING PREMIUMS..."
    SET ws_not_eof TO TRUE
    PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof
        READ insurance_master NEXT
            AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
            NOT AT END
                PERFORM 4210-determine_base_premium
                PERFORM 4220-apply_risk_factor
                PERFORM 4230-calculate_final_premium
        
    
    """"""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF, INSURANCE_MASTER"""
    print("CALCULATING PREMIUMS...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        # Assuming a function to read the next insurance_master record
        INSURANCE_MASTER = read_insurance_master_next()  # type: ignore
        if INSURANCE_MASTER is None:
            WS_EOF = True
        else:
            determine_base_premium_C2()
            apply_risk_factor_C2()
            calculate_final_premium_C2()

def determine_base_premium_C2() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT, INSURANCE_MASTER
    match True:
        case INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_life:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_coverage_amount / 1000 * WS_LIFE_RATE_PER_1000
        case INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_health:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_HEALTH_BASE_PREMIUM
        case INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_auto:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_AUTO_BASE_PREMIUM
        case INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_home:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_coverage_amount / 1000 * WS_HOME_RATE_PER_1000
        case INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_umbrella:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_UMBRELLA_RATE
        case _:
            pass

def apply_risk_factor_C2() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT, INSURANCE_MASTER
    if INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_claims_count > 2:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * Decimal("1.25")

def calculate_final_premium_C2() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT, WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS, INSURANCE_MASTER
    INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_premium_amount  = None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT

def calculate_position_value_C2() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global INVESTMENT_MASTER
    INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_market_value = INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_quantity * INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_current_price

def calculate_gain_loss_C2() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global INVESTMENT_MASTER
    INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_gain_loss = INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_market_value - (INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_quantity * INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_purchase_price)

def calculate_dividends_C2() -> None:"""
    """5400-calculate_dividends"""
    DISPLAY "CALCULATING DIVIDENDS..."
    SET ws_not_eof TO TRUE
    PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof
        READ investment_master NEXT
            AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
            NOT AT END
                IF inv_dividend_rate > 0
                    PERFORM 5410-compute_dividend
                    PERFORM 5420-post_dividend
                
    
    
    """"""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF, INVESTMENT_MASTER"""
    print("CALCULATING DIVIDENDS...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        # Assuming a function to read the next investment_master record
        INVESTMENT_MASTER = read_investment_master_next()  # type: ignore
        if INVESTMENT_MASTER is None:
            WS_EOF = True
        else:
            if INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_dividend_rate > 0:
                compute_dividend_C2()
                post_dividend_C2()

def compute_dividend_C2() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT, INVESTMENT_MASTER
    WS_CALC_AMOUNT = INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_market_value * INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_dividend_rate / 4

def format_date_C2() -> None:
    """8300-format_date
    STRING ws_temp_date(1:4) DELIMITED SIZE
           '-' DELIMITED SIZE
           ws_temp_date(5:2) DELIMITED SIZE
           '-' DELIMITED SIZE"""
           ws_temp_date(7:2) DELIMITED SIZE"""
           INTO ws_formatted_date"""
    """"""
    global WS_TEMP_DATE, WS_FORMATTED_DATE"""

    SET ws_valid TO TRUE
    IF acct_id  = None  # TODO: was SPACES"""
        SET ws_invalid TO TRUE"""

    WS_VALID = True"""
    if ACCOUNT_MASTER.acct_id == "":
        WS_INVALID = True

def calculate_tax_C2() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT, WS_CALC_TAX, WS_BRACKET_1_MAX, WS_BRACKET_1_RATE, WS_BRACKET_2_MAX, WS_BRACKET_2_RATE, WS_BRACKET_3_MAX, WS_BRACKET_3_RATE, WS_BRACKET_5_RATE
    match True:
        case WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_1_MAX:
            WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE
        case WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_2_MAX:
            WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE)
        case WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_3_MAX:
            WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + ((WS_BRACKET_2_MAX - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE) + ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_2_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_3_RATE)
        case _:
            WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_5_RATE

def display_statistics_C2() -> None:"""
    """9200-display_statistics"""
    DISPLAY "============================================"
    DISPLAY "       PROCESSING STATISTICS                "
    DISPLAY "============================================"
    MOVE ws_cust_count TO ws_formatted_count
    DISPLAY "CUSTOMERS PROCESSED:    " ws_formatted_count
    MOVE ws_acct_count TO ws_formatted_count
    DISPLAY "ACCOUNTS PROCESSED:     " ws_formatted_count
    MOVE ws_tran_count TO ws_formatted_count
    DISPLAY "TRANSACTIONS PROCESSED: " ws_formatted_count
    MOVE ws_loan_count TO ws_formatted_count
    DISPLAY "LOANS PROCESSED:        " ws_formatted_count
    MOVE ws_error_count TO ws_formatted_count
    DISPLAY "ERRORS ENCOUNTERED:     " ws_formatted_count
    DISPLAY "============================================"
    MOVE ws_total_deposits TO ws_formatted_amount
    DISPLAY "TOTAL DEPOSITS:    " ws_formatted_amount
    MOVE ws_total_withdrawals TO ws_formatted_amount
    DISPLAY "TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: " ws_formatted_amount
    MOVE ws_total_interest TO ws_formatted_amount
    DISPLAY "TOTAL INTEREST:    " ws_formatted_amount

@dataclass

    cust_last_name: str = ""

    cust_total_loans: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    cust_total_investments: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    cust_last_activity: int = 0  # Assuming YYYYMMDD format

@dataclass

    acct_min_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

def calculate_clv_C3() -> None:
    """9311-calculate_clv."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, CUSTOMER_MASTER, WS_SAVINGS_RATE, WS_PERSONAL_RATE
    WS_CALC_RESULT = (
        CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_balance * WS_SAVINGS_RATE
        + CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_loans * WS_PERSONAL_RATE
        + CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_investments * Decimal("0.01")
    )

def assign_segment_C3() -> None:
    """9312-assign_segment."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, WS_TEMP_CODE
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > 10000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = "PLATINUM"
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 5000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = "GOLD"
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 1000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = "SILVER"
    else:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = "BRONZE"

def extract_data_A110_C3() -> None:
    """A110-extract_data."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF, WS_PROCESS_COUNT, CUSTOMER_MASTER
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        # Mimic READ customer_master NEXT
        # Assuming a function to get the next customer record
        customer = get_next_customer()  # Replace with actual implementation
        if customer is None:
            WS_EOF = True
        else:
            CUSTOMER_MASTER = customer
            WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1

class TransactionLog:
    """Represents the structure of the transaction_log file."""
    tran_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

@dataclass
class LoanRecord:
    """Represents the structure of a loan record."""
    loan_current_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    loan_paid_off: bool = False

CUST_STATUS: str = ""
WS_TEMP_CODE: str = ""
CUST_SSN: str = ""

WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_TOTAL_LOANS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

WS_TOTAL_FEES: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

def initialization_1000_C5() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    # INITIALIZE ws_work_areas - this depends on what's in ws_work_areas'
    # For demonstration, let's assume it contains string and numeric variables'
    global WS_ERROR_MSG, WS_ABORT_REASON
    WS_ERROR_MSG = ""
    WS_ABORT_REASON = ""

    # INITIALIZE ws_counters
    global WS_TRANS_COUNT, WS_ERROR_COUNT, WS_DEPOSIT_COUNT, WS_WITHDRAWAL_COUNT, WS_TRANSFER_COUNT, WS_INTEREST_COUNT, WS_EXCEPTION_IDX, WS_AUDIT_IDX, WS_REJECTED_BATCH_COUNT, WS_COMMITTED_BATCH_COUNT, WS_ACTUAL_COUNT
    WS_TRANS_COUNT = 0
    WS_ERROR_COUNT = 0
    WS_DEPOSIT_COUNT = 0
    WS_WITHDRAWAL_COUNT = 0
    WS_TRANSFER_COUNT = 0
    WS_INTEREST_COUNT = 0
    WS_EXCEPTION_IDX = 0
    WS_AUDIT_IDX = 0
    WS_REJECTED_BATCH_COUNT = 0
    WS_COMMITTED_BATCH_COUNT = 0
    WS_ACTUAL_COUNT = 0

    # INITIALIZE ws_totals
    global WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS, WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS, WS_TOTAL_TRANSFERS, WS_TOTAL_INTEREST, WS_ACTUAL_TOTAL
    WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TOTAL_TRANSFERS = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TOTAL_INTEREST = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_ACTUAL_TOTAL = Decimal("0.00")

    # MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO ws_current_datetime
    global WS_CURRENT_DATETIME
    WS_CURRENT_DATETIME = datetime.now()

    # MOVE ws_curr_year  TO rpt_year
    # MOVE ws_curr_month TO rpt_month
    # MOVE ws_curr_day   TO rpt_day
    global RPT_YEAR, RPT_MONTH, RPT_DAY
    WS_CURR_YEAR = WS_CURRENT_DATETIME.year
    WS_CURR_MONTH = WS_CURRENT_DATETIME.month
    WS_CURR_DAY = WS_CURRENT_DATETIME.day
    RPT_YEAR  = None  # TODO: was WS_CURR_YEAR
    RPT_MONTH  = None  # TODO: was WS_CURR_MONTH
    RPT_DAY  = None  # TODO: was WS_CURR_DAY

    open_files_1100_C5()
    read_parameters_1200_C5()
    initialize_tables_1300_C5()
    load_reference_data_1400_C5()

def open_files_1100_C5() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global CUSTOMER_FILE, ACCOUNT_FILE, TRANSACTION_FILE, REPORT_FILE, ERROR_FILE, MASTER_FILE
    try:
        #In Python, opening a file in 'r' mode implies INPUT, 'w' implies OUTPUT
        customer_file = open(CUSTOMER_FILE, 'r')
        account_file = open(ACCOUNT_FILE, 'r')
        transaction_file = open(TRANSACTION_FILE, 'r')
        report_file = open(REPORT_FILE, 'w')
        error_file = open(ERROR_FILE, 'w')
        master_file = open(MASTER_FILE, 'r+')  # 'r+' provides both read and write, simulating I-O
        
        customer_file.close()
        account_file.close()
        transaction_file.close()
        report_file.close()
        error_file.close()
        master_file.close()
    except Exception as e:
        global WS_ERROR_MSG, WS_FILE_STATUS
        WS_FILE_STATUS = "01" # Simulate a non-zero file status
        WS_ERROR_MSG = "FILE OPEN ERROR"
        abort_process_9500_C5()

def read_parameters_1200_C5() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_PARAM_DATE, WS_PARAM_TIME, WS_JOB_ID, WS_ENV_TYPE, WS_PROCESS_DATE
    WS_PARAM_DATE = datetime.now().date()
    WS_PARAM_TIME = datetime.now().time()
    WS_JOB_ID = 'batch_001'
    WS_ENV_TYPE = 'PRODUCTION'
    # Simulating integer_of_date for demonstration (can be optimized)
    WS_PROCESS_DATE = WS_PARAM_DATE.toordinal()

def initialize_tables_1300_C5() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global RATE_TABLE_ENTRY, BRANCH_TABLE_ENTRY, WS_TBL_IDX
    RATE_TABLE_ENTRY = []
    for WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 101):
        entry = RateTableEntry()
        entry.RT_RATE = Decimal("0.00")
        entry.RT_CODE = ""
        RATE_TABLE_ENTRY.append(entry)
    
    BRANCH_TABLE_ENTRY = []
    for WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 51):
        entry = BranchTableEntry()
        BRANCH_TABLE_ENTRY.append(entry)

def load_reference_data_1400_C5() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_TBL_IDX, WS_EOF_FLAG, REFERENCE_FILE, RATE_TABLE_ENTRY
    WS_TBL_IDX = 1
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    try:
        with open(REFERENCE_FILE, 'r') as ref_file:
            for line in ref_file:
                if WS_EOF_FLAG == 'Y' or WS_TBL_IDX > 100:
                    break
                #Simulate reading ws_ref_code and ws_ref_rate from line
                parts = line.strip().split(',')
                if len(parts) == 2:
                    WS_REF_CODE = parts[0]
                    WS_REF_RATE = Decimal(parts[1])

                    if WS_TBL_IDX <= len(RATE_TABLE_ENTRY):
                      RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_IDX - 1].RT_CODE  = None  # TODO: was WS_REF_CODE
                      RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_IDX - 1].RT_RATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_REF_RATE
                    else:
                       #Handle the case if the table size is exceeded
                       print(f"Warning: Table size exceeded at index {WS_TBL_IDX}")
                       WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
                       break

                    WS_TBL_IDX += 1
                else:
                    #Handle incomplete data
                    print("Warning: Incomplete reference data")

    except FileNotFoundError:
        print("Error: reference_file not found")
        WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"An error occurred: {e}")
        WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def process_transactions_2000_C5() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""TODO"""TODO"""TODO"""

class WsLoanProcessingArea:
    """01  ws_loan_processing_area."""
    ws_loan_id: str = ""  # PIC X(15)
    ws_loan_type: str = ""  # PIC X(03)

@dataclass
class WsLoanRecord:
    """COBOL: ws_loan_record"""
    loan_rec_id: str = ''
    loan_rec_type: str = ''
    loan_rec_amount: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')

    loan_rec_status: str = ''

    hold_purchase_date: int = 0
    hold_type: str = ''

    """COBOL: CALL 'GETQUOTE'"""
    # Dummy implementation: Replace with actual quote retrieval logic

        return QuoteResponse(quote_response_status='OK', quote_last_price=Decimal('50.00'))
    else:
        return QuoteResponse(quote_response_status='ERROR', quote_last_price=Decimal('0.00'))

    def calculate_pmi_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10335-calculate_pmi."""
        if self.WS_LTV_RATIO > 95:
            self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal('0.0125') / 12
        elif self.WS_LTV_RATIO > 90:
            self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal('0.0100') / 12
        elif self.WS_LTV_RATIO > 85:
            self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal('0.0075') / 12
        else:
            self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal('0.0050') / 12

    def evaluate_history_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10340-evaluate_history."""
        if self.WS_LATE_90_DAYS > 0:
            self.WS_RISK_SCORE -= 50
            self.WS_FACTOR_1 = 'SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY'
        if self.WS_LATE_60_DAYS > 2:
            self.WS_RISK_SCORE -= 30
            self.WS_FACTOR_2 = '60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES'
        if self.WS_LATE_30_DAYS > 5:
            self.WS_RISK_SCORE -= 20
            self.WS_FACTOR_3 = 'MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES'

    def calculate_final_risk_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10350-calculate_final_risk."""
        self.WS_RISK_SCORE = self.WS_RISK_SCORE // 4
        if self.WS_RISK_SCORE >= 80:
            self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'LOW RISK'
        elif self.WS_RISK_SCORE >= 60:
            self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'MODERATE'
        elif self.WS_RISK_SCORE >= 40:
            self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'ELEVATED'
        else:
            self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'HIGH RISK'

    def determine_approval_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10400-determine_approval."""
        if self.WS_CREDIT_TIER == 'F':
            self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'D'
            self.WS_CONDITIONS = 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW'
            return
        if self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY == 'HIGH RISK':
            self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'D'
            self.WS_CONDITIONS = 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED'
            return
        if self.WS_DTI_RATIO > 50:
            self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'D'
            self.WS_CONDITIONS = 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH'
            return
        self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'A'
        self.calculate_approved_terms_C7()

    def calculate_approved_terms_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10450-calculate_approved_terms."""
        self.WS_APPROVED_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
        match self.WS_CREDIT_TIER:
            case 'A':
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal('0.00')
            case 'B':
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal('0.50')
            case 'C':
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal('1.50')
            case 'D':
                self.WS_APPROVED_RATE = self.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal('3.00')
        if self.WS_RISK_CATEGORY == 'ELEVATED':
            self.WS_APPROVED_RATE += Decimal('0.50')

    def generate_loan_terms_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10500-generate_loan_terms."""
        self.WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE = self.WS_APPROVED_RATE
        self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE = self.WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE / 1200
        self.WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR = (1 + self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE) ** self.WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS
        self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT = (self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE * 0  # TODO
                                    self.WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR / (self.WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR - 1))
        self.WS_LOAN_PRINCIPAL_BAL = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT

    def create_amortization_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10600-create_amortization."""
        self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
        self.WS_PAYMENT_DATE = int(datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d"))  # Assuming current_date returns YYYYMMDD
        self.WS_AMORT_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_AMORT_IDX <= self.WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS:
            self.calculate_payment_split_C7()
            self.WS_AMORT_IDX += 1

    def calculate_payment_split_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10650-calculate_payment_split."""
        self.AMORT_INTEREST[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = (self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE * 0  # TODO
                                                  self.WS_MONTHLY_RATE)
        self.AMORT_PRINCIPAL[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = (self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT - 0  # TODO
                                                    self.AMORT_INTEREST[self.WS_AMORT_IDX])
        self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE -= self.AMORT_PRINCIPAL[self.WS_AMORT_IDX]
        self.AMORT_BALANCE[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = self.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE
        self.AMORT_PAYMENT_NUM[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = self.WS_AMORT_IDX
        self.AMORT_PAYMENT_AMT[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT
        if self.loan_mortgage.loan_mortgage:
            self.AMORT_ESCROW[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = ((self.WS_PROPERTY_TAX + 0  # TODO
                                                       self.WS_INSURANCE_PREMIUM) / 12)
            self.AMORT_TOTAL_PMT[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = (self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT + 0  # TODO
                                                        self.AMORT_ESCROW[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] + 0  # TODO
                                                        self.WS_PMI_AMOUNT)
        else:
            self.AMORT_TOTAL_PMT[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT
        self.advance_payment_date_C7()

    def advance_payment_date_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10660-advance_payment_date."""
        self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH += 1
        if self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH > 12:
            self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH = 1
            self.WS_PAYMENT_YEAR += 1
        self.AMORT_PAYMENT_DATE[self.WS_AMORT_IDX] = (self.WS_PAYMENT_YEAR * 10000 + 0  # TODO
                                                      self.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH * 100 + 1)

    def finalize_loan_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10700-finalize_loan."""
        self.WS_LOAN_START_DATE = int(datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d"))  # Assuming current_date returns YYYYMMDD
        self.WS_LOAN_END_DATE = self.WS_LOAN_START_DATE + (self.WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS * 30)
        self.WS_LOAN_STATUS = 'A'
        self.create_loan_record_C7()
        self.disburse_funds_C7()
        self.send_confirmation_C7()

    def create_loan_record_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10750-create_loan_record."""
        self.ws_loan_record = WsLoanRecord()  # Equivalent to INITIALIZE ws_loan_record
        self.ws_loan_record.loan_rec_id = self.WS_LOAN_ID
        self.ws_loan_record.loan_rec_type = self.WS_LOAN_TYPE
        self.ws_loan_record.loan_rec_amount = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
        self.ws_loan_record.loan_rec_rate = self.WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE
        self.ws_loan_record.loan_rec_payment = self.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT
        self.ws_loan_record.loan_rec_start = self.WS_LOAN_START_DATE
        self.ws_loan_record.loan_rec_status = self.WS_LOAN_STATUS
        write_loan_record(self.ws_loan_record)  # Assuming write_loan_record is defined elsewhere

    def disburse_funds_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10760-disburse_funds."""
        self.WS_DISBURSEMENT_AMOUNT = self.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
        process_deposit(self.WS_DISBURSEMENT_AMOUNT) # Assuming process_deposit is defined elsewhere
        write_audit_trail() # Assuming write_audit_trail is defined elsewhere

    def send_confirmation_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10770-send_confirmation."""
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'loan_confirm'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Your loan has been approved'
        send_notification(self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE, self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT) # Assuming send_notification is defined elsewhere

    def process_decline_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10800-process_decline."""
        self.WS_LOAN_STATUS = 'DECLINED'
        self.record_decline_C7()
        self.send_decline_notice_C7()

    def record_decline_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10810-record_decline."""
        self.ws_decline_record = WsDeclineRecord() # Equivalent to INITIALIZE ws_decline_record
        self.ws_decline_record.decline_loan_id = self.WS_LOAN_ID
        self.ws_decline_record.decline_status = self.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS
        self.ws_decline_record.decline_reason = self.WS_CONDITIONS
        self.ws_decline_record.decline_date = int(datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")) # Assuming current_date returns YYYYMMDD
        write_decline_record(self.ws_decline_record) # Assuming write_decline_record is defined elsewhere

    def send_decline_notice_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 10820-send_decline_notice."""
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'loan_decline'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'LETTER'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Regarding your loan application'
        send_notification(self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE, self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT) # Assuming send_notification is defined elsewhere

    def portfolio_management_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 11000-portfolio_management."""
        self.load_portfolio_C7()
        self.update_market_prices_C7()
        self.calculate_values_C7()
        self.rebalance_check_C7()
        self.generate_statements_C7()

    def load_portfolio_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 11100-load_portfolio."""
        self.WS_HOLD_IDX = 1
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = ''
        while self.WS_HOLD_IDX <= 100 and self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
            ws_holding_rec = read_holdings_file()
            if ws_holding_rec.hold_symbol == '': # Simulate AT END
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
            else:
                self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX] = ws_holding_rec
                self.WS_HOLD_IDX += 1
        self.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT = self.WS_HOLD_IDX - 1

    def update_market_prices_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 11200-update_market_prices."""
        self.WS_HOLD_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_HOLD_IDX <= self.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT:
            self.WS_QUOTE_SYMBOL = self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_symbol
            self.get_quote_C7()
            self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_current_price = self.WS_QUOTE_PRICE
            self.WS_HOLD_IDX += 1

    def get_quote_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 11250-get_quote."""
        self.quote_request = QuoteRequest(quote_request_symbol=self.WS_QUOTE_SYMBOL)
        self.quote_response = getquote(self.quote_request)
        if self.quote_response.quote_response_status == 'OK':
            self.WS_QUOTE_PRICE = self.quote_response.quote_last_price
        else:
            self.WS_QUOTE_PRICE = Decimal('0.00')

    def calculate_values_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 11300-calculate_values."""
        self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_COST_BASIS = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_UNREALIZED_GAIN = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_HOLD_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_HOLD_IDX <= self.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT:
            self.calculate_holding_value_C7()
            self.WS_HOLD_IDX += 1

    def calculate_holding_value_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 11350-calculate_holding_value."""
        self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_market_value = (self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_shares * 0  # TODO
                                                                self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_current_price)
        self.WS_HOLD_COST = (self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_shares * 0  # TODO
                             self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_cost_per_share)
        self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_gain_loss = (self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_market_value - 0  # TODO
                                                             self.WS_HOLD_COST)
        if self.WS_HOLD_COST > 0:
            self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_pct_change = ((self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_gain_loss / 0  # TODO
                                                                   self.WS_HOLD_COST) * 100)
        else:
            self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_pct_change = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_TOTAL_VALUE += self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_market_value
        self.WS_COST_BASIS += self.WS_HOLD_COST
        self.WS_UNREALIZED_GAIN += self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_gain_loss

    def rebalance_check_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 11400-rebalance_check."""
        self.calculate_current_allocation_C7()
        self.compare_to_target_C7()
        if self.WS_REBALANCE_NEEDED == 'Y':
            self.generate_rebalance_trades_C7()

    def calculate_current_allocation_C7(self) -> None:
        """COBOL: 11410-calculate_current_allocation."""
        self.WS_STOCKS_VALUE = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_BONDS_VALUE = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_CASH_VALUE = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_HOLD_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_HOLD_IDX <= self.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT:
            match self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_type:
                case 'STK':
                    self.WS_STOCKS_VALUE += self.WS_HOLDING[self.WS_HOLD_IDX].hold_market_value
                case 'BND'

def calc_premiums_C8(data_storage: DataStorage) -> None:
    """Top level premium calculation"""
    calc_auto_premium_C8(data_storage)
    calc_home_premium_C8(data_storage)
    calc_health_premium_C8(data_storage)

def calc_auto_premium_C8(data_storage: DataStorage) -> None:
    """13220-calc_auto_premium."""COBOL logic"""13230-calc_home_premium."""COBOL logic"""13240-calc_health_premium."""COBOL logic"""13300-UNDERWRITING."""COBOL logic"""13310-evaluate_risk_factors."""COBOL logic"""13320-check_medical_history."""COBOL logic"""13330-verify_information."""COBOL logic"""13335-check_fraud_indicators."""COBOL logic"""13336-validate_documents."""COBOL logic"""13340-determine_decision."""COBOL logic"""13400-issue_policy."""COBOL logic"""13410-generate_policy_number."""
    data_storage.WS_DATE_PART = datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d") # MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO ws_date_part
    data_storage.WS_TYPE_PART = data_storage.WS_POLICY_TYPE # MOVE ws_policy_type TO ws_type_part
    data_storage.WS_RANDOM_PART = int(random.random() * 99999) # COMPUTE ws_random_part = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999
    data_storage.WS_POLICY_NUMBER = f"{data_storage.WS_TYPE_PART}{data_storage.WS_DATE_PART}{data_storage.WS_RANDOM_PART}" # STRING ws_type_part DELIMITED SIZE ws_date_part DELIMITED SIZE ws_random_part DELIMITED SIZE INTO ws_policy_number
def create_policy_record_C8(data_storage: DataStorage) -> None:
    """13420-create_policy_record."""COBOL logic"""13430-set_beneficiaries."""COBOL logic"""13440-send_policy_docs."""
    data_storage.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'policy_issue' # MOVE 'policy_issue' TO ws_notif_type
    data_storage.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'MAIL' # MOVE 'MAIL' TO ws_notif_channel
    data_storage.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f"Your policy {data_storage.WS_POLICY_NUMBER} has been issued" # STRING 'Your policy ' DELIMITED SIZE ws_policy_number DELIMITED SIZE ' has been issued' DELIMITED SIZE INTO ws_notif_subject
    send_notification_C8(data_storage) # PERFORM 15000-send_notification

class Time:
    hour: int
    minute: int
    second: int

def CURRENT_DATE() -> Date:
    """Mimic COBOL's current_date function"""
    now = datetime.datetime.now()
    return Date(year=now.year, month=now.month, day=now.day)

def CURRENT_TIME() -> Time:
    """Mimic COBOL's current_time function"""
    now = datetime.datetime.now()
    return Time(hour=now.hour, minute=now.minute, second=now.second)

def INTEGER_OF_DATE(date: Date) -> int:
    """Mimic COBOL's integer_of_date function"""
    return date.year * 10000 + date.month * 100 + date.day

class DataItems:

data_items = DataItems()

# File stubs (replace with actual file I/O)
def WRITE(record: Any, filename: str) -> None:
    """Stub for WRITE statement."""
    print(f"WRITE {record} to {filename}")

def REWRITE(filename: str) -> None:
    """Stub for REWRITE statement."""
    print(f"REWRITE {filename}")

def READ(filename: str, key: str) -> Any:
    """Stub for READ statement."""
    print(f"READ {filename} with key {key}")
    return None

def STRING(*args, DELIMITED: str, INTO: str) -> None:
    """Mimic COBOL's STRING function"""
    result = ""
    for arg in args:
        if arg != "DELIMITED" and arg != "SIZE":
            result += str(arg)
    if INTO == "ws_case_id":
        global_vars.ws_case_id = result
    elif INTO == "ws_doc_id":
        global_vars.ws_doc_id = result
    elif INTO == "ws_workflow_id":
        global_vars.ws_workflow_id = result
    else:
        print(f"STRING: Unhandled INTO variable {INTO}")

def INITIALIZE(record: str) -> None:
    """Mimic COBOL's INITIALIZE statement"""
    if record == "ws_escalation_record":
        global_vars.esc_reason = ""
        global_vars.esc_customer = ""
        global_vars.esc_date = Date(year=1900, month=1, day=1)
        global_vars.esc_priority = ""
    elif record == "ws_sar_record":
        data_items.sar_rec_name = ""
        data_items.sar_rec_addr = ""
        data_items.sar_rec_amount = Decimal("0.00")
        data_items.sar_rec_date = Date(year=1900, month=1, day=1)
        data_items.sar_rec_narrative = ""
    elif record == "ws_credit_record":
        data_items.credit_account = ""
        data_items.credit_amount = Decimal("0.00")
        data_items.credit_reason = ""
    elif record == "ws_card_request":
        data_items.card_req_account = ""
        data_items.card_req_type = ""
        data_items.card_req_expedite = ""
    elif record == "ws_reset_request":
        data_items.reset_customer = ""
        data_items.reset_type = ""
    elif record == "ws_case_update":
        data_items.case_upd_id = ""
        data_items.case_upd_status = ""
        data_items.case_upd_resolution = ""
        data_items.case_upd_close_date = Date(year=1900, month=1, day=1)
    elif record == "ws_callback_record":
        data_items.callback_case = ""
        data_items.callback_phone = ""
        data_items.callback_date = Date(year=1900, month=1, day=1)
    elif record == "ws_storage_request":
        data_items.store_doc_id = ""
        data_items.store_bucket = ""
        data_items.store_size = 0
    elif record == "ws_metrics_record":
        data_items.metrics_workflow_id = ""
        data_items.metrics_type = ""
        data_items.metrics_status = ""
        data_items.metrics_duration = 0
    elif record == "ws_batch_log":
        data_items.log_batch_id = ""
        data_items.log_status = ""
        data_items.log_start = Date(year=1900, month=1, day=1)
        data_items.log_end = Date(year=1900, month=1, day=1)
        data_items.log_records = 0
        data_items.log_rc = 0
    else:
        print(f"INITIALIZE: Unhandled record {record}")

@dataclass
class WsDailySummary:
    """COBOL: ws_daily_summary"""
    daily_date: str = ""
    daily_trans_count: int = 0
    daily_trans_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    daily_deposits: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    daily_withdrawals: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

@dataclass
class WsWeeklySummary:
    """COBOL: ws_weekly_summary"""
    weekly_week: int = 0
    weekly_trans_count: int = 0
    weekly_trans_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

@dataclass
class WsMonthlySummary:
    """COBOL: ws_monthly_summary"""
    monthly_month: int = 0
    monthly_year: int = 0
    monthly_trans_count: int = 0
    monthly_trans_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    monthly_new_accounts: int = 0
    monthly_closed_accounts: int = 0

@dataclass
class WsEscheatRecord:
    """COBOL: ws_escheat_record"""
    escheat_account: str = ""
    escheat_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    escheat_date: str = ""
    escheat_owner: str = ""
    escheat_address: str = ""

@dataclass

    archive_retention: int = 0

@dataclass
class WsCardRecord:
    """COBOL: ws_card_record"""

    card_network: str = ""
    card_daily_limit: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    card_atm_limit: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

@dataclass
class WsShipmentRecord:
    """COBOL: ws_shipment_record"""

    ship_address: str = ""

def data_analytics_C10():
    """COBOL: 21000-data_analytics."""COBOL logic"""COBOL: 21100-collect_metrics."""COBOL logic"""COBOL: 21110-collect_transaction_metrics."""
    global WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT, WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT, WS_AVG_TRANS_AMOUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG

    WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal("0.00")  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO ws_total_trans_amount
    WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT = 0  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO ws_total_trans_count
    WS_AVG_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal("0.00")  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO ws_avg_trans_amount

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # COBOL: PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        trans_rec = read_transaction_file() # COBOL: READ transaction_file INTO ws_trans_rec
        if trans_rec is None:  # COBOL: AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # COBOL: MOVE 'Y' TO ws_eof_flag
        else:  # COBOL: NOT AT END
            WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT += 1  # COBOL: ADD 1 TO ws_total_trans_count
            WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT += trans_rec.trans_amount  # COBOL: ADD trans_amount TO ws_total_trans_amount

    if WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT > 0:  # COBOL: IF ws_total_trans_count > 0
        WS_AVG_TRANS_AMOUNT = WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT / WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT  # COBOL: COMPUTE ws_avg_trans_amount = ws_total_trans_amount / ws_total_trans_count

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO ws_eof_flag

def collect_customer_metrics_C10():
    """COBOL: 21120-collect_customer_metrics."""COBOL logic"""COBOL: 21130-collect_performance_metrics."""
    global WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL, WS_RESPONSE_COUNT, WS_AVG_RESPONSE_TIME, WS_EOF_FLAG

    WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL = Decimal("0.00")  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO ws_response_time_total
    WS_RESPONSE_COUNT = 0  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO ws_response_count

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # COBOL: PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        perf_rec = read_perf_log_file()  # COBOL: READ perf_log_file INTO ws_perf_rec
        if perf_rec is None:  # COBOL: AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # COBOL: MOVE 'Y' TO ws_eof_flag
        else:  # COBOL: NOT AT END
            WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL += perf_rec.perf_response_time  # COBOL: ADD perf_response_time TO ws_response_time_total
            WS_RESPONSE_COUNT += 1  # COBOL: ADD 1 TO ws_response_count

    if WS_RESPONSE_COUNT > 0:  # COBOL: IF ws_response_count > 0
        WS_AVG_RESPONSE_TIME = WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL / WS_RESPONSE_COUNT  # COBOL: COMPUTE ws_avg_response_time = ws_response_time_total / ws_response_count

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO ws_eof_flag

def aggregate_data_C10():
    """COBOL: 21200-aggregate_data."""COBOL logic"""COBOL: 21210-daily_aggregation."""COBOL logic"""COBOL: 21220-weekly_aggregation."""COBOL logic"""COBOL: 21225-sum_week_data."""
    global WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY
    
    WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY.weekly_trans_count = 0  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO weekly_trans_count
    WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY.weekly_trans_amount = Decimal("0.00")  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO weekly_trans_amount
    
    for _ in range(7):  # COBOL: PERFORM 7 TIMES
        daily_summary = read_daily_summary_file()
        if daily_summary is not None:
            WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY.weekly_trans_count += daily_summary.daily_trans_count  # COBOL: ADD daily_trans_count TO weekly_trans_count
            WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY.weekly_trans_amount += daily_summary.daily_trans_amount  # COBOL: ADD daily_trans_amount TO weekly_trans_amount

def monthly_aggregation_C10():
    """COBOL: 21230-monthly_aggregation."""COBOL logic"""COBOL: 21235-sum_month_data."""
    global WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY, WS_EOF_FLAG

    WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY.monthly_trans_count = 0  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO monthly_trans_count
    WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY.monthly_trans_amount = Decimal("0.00")  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO monthly_trans_amount
    WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY.monthly_new_accounts = 0  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO monthly_new_accounts
    WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY.monthly_closed_accounts = 0  # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO monthly_closed_accounts

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # COBOL: PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        daily_sum_rec = read_daily_summary_file()  # COBOL: READ daily_summary_file INTO ws_daily_sum_rec
        if daily_sum_rec is None:  # COBOL: AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # COBOL: MOVE 'Y' TO ws_eof_flag
        else:  # COBOL: NOT AT END
            if daily_sum_rec.daily_month == WS_CURR_MONTH:  # COBOL: IF daily_month = ws_curr_month
                WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY.monthly_trans_count += daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count  # COBOL: ADD daily_trans_count TO monthly_trans_count
                WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY.monthly_trans_amount += daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount  # COBOL: ADD daily_trans_amount TO monthly_trans_amount

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO ws_eof_flag

def calculate_kpi_C10():

class AccessLog:
    access_box_number: Optional[int] = None
    access_customer: Optional[str] = None
    access_date: Optional[int] = None
    access_time: Optional[str] = None
    access_type: Optional[str] = None

@dataclass
class DrillingRecord:
    drill_box_number: Optional[int] = None
    drill_reason: Optional[str] = None
    drill_scheduled_date: Optional[int] = None

@dataclass
class AuthRecord:
    auth_rec_card: Optional[str] = None
    auth_rec_amount: Optional[Decimal] = None
    auth_rec_code: Optional[int] = None
    auth_rec_date: Optional[int] = None
    auth_rec_time: Optional[str] = None
    auth_rec_merchant: Optional[str] = None
    auth_rec_status: Optional[str] = None

@dataclass
class DeclineRecord:
    decline_rec_card: Optional[str] = None
    decline_rec_amount: Optional[Decimal] = None
    decline_rec_code: Optional[str] = None
    decline_rec_date: Optional[int] = None

@dataclass
class CaptureRecord:
    capture_card: Optional[str] = None
    capture_amount: Optional[Decimal] = None
    capture_auth_code: Optional[int] = None
    capture_date: Optional[int] = None
    capture_settled: Optional[str] = None

@dataclass
class FundingRecord:
    funding_merchant: Optional[str] = None
    funding_amount: Optional[Decimal] = None
    funding_fees: Optional[Decimal] = None
    funding_date: Optional[int] = None

@dataclass
class SettleHeader:
    settle_record_type: Optional[str] = None
    settle_merchant_id: Optional[str] = None
    settle_date: Optional[int] = None

@dataclass
class SettleDetail:
    settle_record_type: Optional[str] = None
    settle_card: Optional[str] = None
    settle_amount: Optional[Decimal] = None
    settle_auth_code: Optional[int] = None

@dataclass
class SettleTrailer:
    settle_record_type: Optional[str] = None
    settle_total_count: Optional[int] = None
    settle_total_amount: Optional[Decimal] = None

@dataclass
class ChargebackRecord:
    cb_card: Optional[str] = None
    cb_amount: Optional[Decimal] = None
    cb_reason: Optional[str] = None
    cb_case_id: Optional[str] = None
    cb_received_date: Optional[int] = None
    cb_status: Optional[str] = None
    cb_action: Optional[str] = None

@dataclass
class FileErrorLog:
    file_err_name: Optional[str] = None
    file_err_status: Optional[str] = None"""

def validate_stop_request_C12() -> None:
    """29100-validate_stop_request."""
    global WS_STOP_VALID, WS_STOP_REJECT
    WS_STOP_VALID = 'Y'
    if WS_CHECK_NUMBER == 0:
        WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'
        WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK NUMBER REQUIRED'
    if WS_CHECK_ALREADY_CLEARED == 'Y':
        WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'
        WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED'

    WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_status = 'A'

    # WRITE stop_record FROM ws_stop_record.  (Placeholder - file I/O not implemented)

        check_availability_C12()

            assign_box_C12()
            create_rental_agreement_C12()

            if WS_KEY_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'Y'

            WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'

def box_billing_C12() -> None:
    """30400-box_billing."""
    ws_box_idx = 1
    while ws_box_idx <= WS_TOTAL_BOXES:
        if BOX_STATUS[ws_box_idx - 1] == 'R':
            if BOX_RENEWAL_DUE[ws_box_idx - 1] == 'Y':
                charge_annual_fee_C12(ws_box_idx)
        ws_box_idx += 1

def charge_annual_fee_C12(ws_box_idx: int) -> None:
    """30410-charge_annual_fee."""
    global WS_CUSTOMER_ID, WS_FEE_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
    WS_CUSTOMER_ID = BOX_RENTER[ws_box_idx - 1]
    WS_FEE_AMOUNT = BOX_ANNUAL_FEE[ws_box_idx - 1]
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_FEE_AMOUNT
    update_account_C12()
    BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[ws_box_idx - 1] = BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[ws_box_idx - 1] + 10000

def merchant_services_C12() -> None:
    """31000-merchant_services."""
    process_authorization_C12()
    capture_transaction_C12()
    process_settlement_C12()
    handle_chargeback_C12()

def process_authorization_C12() -> None:
    """31100-process_authorization."""
    validate_card_C12()
    if WS_CARD_VALID == 'Y':
        check_fraud_score_C12()
        if WS_FRAUD_APPROVED == 'Y':
            check_available_credit_C12()
            if WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE == 'Y':
                approve_auth_C12()
            else:
                decline_auth_C12()
        else:
            decline_auth_C12()
    else:
        decline_auth_C12()

def validate_card_C12() -> None:
    """31110-validate_card."""
    global WS_CARD_VALID
    WS_CARD_VALID = 'N'
    check_luhn_C12()
    if WS_LUHN_VALID == 'Y':
        check_expiry_C12()
        if WS_NOT_EXPIRED == 'Y':
            check_cvv_C12()
            if WS_CVV_VALID == 'Y':
                WS_CARD_VALID = 'Y'

def check_luhn_C12() -> None:
    """31115-check_luhn."""
    global WS_LUHN_SUM, WS_LUHN_VALID
    WS_LUHN_SUM = 0
    ws_luhn_idx = 16
    while ws_luhn_idx >= 1:
        ws_luhn_digit = int(WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER[ws_luhn_idx - 1])
        if (17 - ws_luhn_idx) % 2 == 0:
            ws_luhn_digit *= 2
            if ws_luhn_digit > 9:
                ws_luhn_digit -= 9
        WS_LUHN_SUM += ws_luhn_digit
        ws_luhn_idx -= 1
    if WS_LUHN_SUM % 10 == 0:
        WS_LUHN_VALID = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_LUHN_VALID = 'N'

def check_expiry_C12() -> None:
    """31116-check_expiry."""
    global WS_NOT_EXPIRED
    if WS_AUTH_EXPIRY_DATE >= WS_PROCESS_DATE:
        WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'N'

def check_cvv_C12() -> None:
    """31117-check_cvv."""
    global WS_CVV_VALID, WS_CVV_RESULT
    cvvverify_C12(WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, WS_AUTH_CVV) # CALL 'CVVVERIFY' USING ws_auth_card_number ws_auth_cvv ws_cvv_result
    if WS_CVV_RESULT == 'M':
        WS_CVV_VALID = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_CVV_VALID = 'N'

def check_fraud_score_C12() -> None:
    """31120-check_fraud_score."""
    global WS_FRAUD_APPROVED, WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE
    fraudcheck_C12(WS_AUTH_REQUEST) # CALL 'FRAUDCHECK' USING ws_auth_request ws_fraud_response
    if FRAUD_SCORE < 70:
        WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'N'
        WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE  = None  # TODO: was FRAUD_DECLINE_CODE

def check_available_credit_C12() -> None:
    """31130-check_available_credit."""
    global WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE, WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
    # READ card_account_file INTO ws_card_account_rec (Placeholder - file I/O not implemented)
    WS_CARD_ACCOUNT_REC = {} # Dummy read
    if WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT >= WS_AUTH_AMOUNT:
        WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'N'
        WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = '51'

def capture_transaction_C12() -> None:

def validate_auth_code_C12() -> None:
    """31210-validate_auth_code."""
    global WS_AUTH_VALID, WS_AUTH_REC, AUTH_REC_STATUS
    WS_AUTH_VALID = 'N'
    AUTH_SEARCH_KEY = WS_CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE
    # READ auth_file INTO ws_auth_rec KEY IS auth_code (Placeholder - file I/O not implemented)
    WS_AUTH_REC = {}  # Dummy read
    if WS_AUTH_REC == {}:  # INVALID KEY
        WS_AUTH_VALID = 'N'
    else: # NOT INVALID KEY
        if AUTH_REC_STATUS == 'P':
            WS_AUTH_VALID = 'Y'

    AUTH_REC_STATUS = 'C'

    WS_CAPTURE_RECORD = CaptureRecord()
    WS_CAPTURE_RECORD.capture_card  = None  # TODO: was AUTH_REC_CARD
    WS_CAPTURE_RECORD.capture_amount  = None  # TODO: was WS_CAPTURE_AMOUNT
    WS_CAPTURE_RECORD.capture_auth_code = WS_CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE
    WS_CAPTURE_RECORD.capture_date  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    CAPTURE_RECORD  = None  # TODO: was WS_CAPTURE_RECORD
    # WRITE capture_record FROM ws_capture_record. (Placeholder - file I/O not implemented)

def process_settlement_C12() -> None:
    """31300-process_settlement."""
    batch_transactions_C12()
    calculate_fees_C12()
    create_funding_record_C12()

def batch_transactions_C12() -> None:
    """31310-batch_transactions."""
    global WS_BATCH_TOTAL, WS_BATCH_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_CAPTURE_REC, CAPTURE_SETTLED, CAPTURE_RECORD
    WS_BATCH_TOTAL = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_BATCH_COUNT = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG == 'N':
        # READ capture_file INTO ws_capture_rec (Placeholder - file I/O not implemented)
        if not CAPTURE_FILE:  # AT END
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
            WS_CAPTURE_REC = CaptureRecord()
        else: # NOT AT END
            WS_CAPTURE_REC = CaptureRecord()
            WS_CAPTURE_REC.capture_amount = Decimal("10.00") # Dummy value for testing

            if CAPTURE_SETTLED == 'N':
                WS_BATCH_TOTAL += WS_CAPTURE_REC.capture_amount
                WS_BATCH_COUNT += 1
                CAPTURE_SETTLED = 'Y'
                # REWRITE capture_record FROM ws_capture_rec (Placeholder - file I/O not implemented)

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def calculate_fees_C12() -> None:
    """31320-calculate_fees."""
    global WS_INTERCHANGE_FEE, WS_ASSESSMENT_FEE, WS_PROCESSOR_FEE, WS_TOTAL_FEES
    WS_INTERCHANGE_FEE = WS_BATCH_TOTAL * Decimal("0.0175")
    WS_ASSESSMENT_FEE = WS_BATCH_TOTAL * Decimal("0.0015")
    WS_PROCESSOR_FEE = Decimal(WS_BATCH_COUNT) * Decimal("0.10")
    WS_TOTAL_FEES = WS_INTERCHANGE_FEE + WS_ASSESSMENT_FEE + WS_PROCESSOR_FEE

def create_funding_record_C12() -> None:
    """31330-create_funding_record."""
    global WS_NET_FUNDING, WS_FUNDING_RECORD, FUNDING_RECORD
    WS_NET_FUNDING = WS_BATCH_TOTAL - WS_TOTAL_FEES
    WS_FUNDING_RECORD = FundingRecord()
    WS_FUNDING_RECORD.funding_merchant  = None  # TODO: was WS_MERCHANT_ID
    WS_FUNDING_RECORD.funding_amount  = None  # TODO: was WS_NET_FUNDING
    WS_FUNDING_RECORD.funding_fees  = None  # TODO: was WS_TOTAL_FEES
    WS_FUNDING_RECORD.funding_date = integer_of_date_C12(WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 2
    FUNDING_RECORD  = None  # TODO: was WS_FUNDING_RECORD
    # WRITE funding_record FROM ws_funding_record. (Placeholder - file I/O not implemented)
    print(f"Writing funding record: {WS_FUNDING_RECORD}") # Simulate write

def calculate_cash_position_C13() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_CASH_POSITION
    WS_CASH_POSITION = Decimal('0.00')
    sum_vault_cash_C13()
    sum_fed_account_C13()
    sum_correspondent_balances_C13()


def sum_vault_cash_C13() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_CASH_POSITION, VAULT_BALANCE

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            # Simulate reading from file and populate WS_VAULT_REC and VAULT_BALANCE
            # In a real implementation, this would involve file I/O
            WS_VAULT_REC = {"vault_balance": Decimal("1000.00")}  # Example data
            VAULT_BALANCE = WS_VAULT_REC["vault_balance"]

            WS_CASH_POSITION += None  # TODO: was VAULT_BALANCE
        except FileNotFoundError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        except Exception as e:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
            print(f"Error reading vault cash file: {e}")

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'


def sum_fed_account_C13() -> None:
    """
    32120-sum_fed_account"""
        READ fed_account_file INTO ws_fed_balance"""
        ADD ws_fed_balance TO ws_cash_position"""
    """
    global WS_CASH_POSITION, WS_FED_BALANCE"""
    # Simulate reading from file and populate WS_FED_BALANCE"""
    # In a real implementation, this would involve file I/O"""
    WS_FED_BALANCE = Decimal("50000.00")  # Example data
    WS_CASH_POSITION += None  # TODO: was WS_FED_BALANCE

#34000-capital_management
#            PERFORM 34100-calculate_capital_ratios
#            PERFORM 34200-risk_weighted_assets
#            PERFORM 34300-capital_planning
#            PERFORM 34400-stress_testing.
def capital_management_C14(ws_common_stock: Decimal, ws_retained_earnings: Decimal, ws_aoci: Decimal, ws_goodwill: Decimal, ws_intangibles: Decimal, ws_dta_deduction: Decimal, ws_sub_debt: Decimal, ws_alll_eligible: Decimal, ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal, ws_total_assets: Decimal, ws_cash_position: Decimal, ws_govt_securities: Decimal, ws_bank_deposits: Decimal, ws_residential_mortgages: Decimal, ws_commercial_loans: Decimal, ws_consumer_loans: Decimal, ws_trading_assets: Decimal, ws_market_risk_factor: Decimal, ws_gross_income: Decimal, ws_operational_factor: Decimal, ws_growth_rate: Decimal, ws_target_ratio: Decimal, ws_retained_earnings_proj: Decimal, ws_sub_debt_capacity: Decimal, ws_plan_update_date: date, capital_plan_record: File, ws_capital_plan: "WSCapitalPlan", ws_scenario_name: str, ws_rate_shock: Decimal, ws_gdp_change: Decimal, ws_unemployment_rate: Decimal, ws_housing_decline: Decimal, ws_loan_portfolio: Decimal, ws_stress_lgd: Decimal, ws_stress_pd: Decimal, ws_total_capital: Decimal, ws_min_capital_ratio: Decimal, ws_stress_pass_fail: str, ws_notif_type: str, ws_notif_channel: str, ws_notif_subject: str) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, str, str, str]:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_tier1_capital, ws_tier2_capital, ws_total_capital, ws_cet1_ratio, ws_capital_ratio, ws_leverage_ratio = calculate_capital_ratios_C14(ws_common_stock, ws_retained_earnings, ws_aoci, ws_goodwill, ws_intangibles, ws_dta_deduction, ws_sub_debt, ws_alll_eligible, ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_total_assets)
    ws_risk_weighted_assets = risk_weighted_assets_C14(ws_cash_position, ws_govt_securities, ws_bank_deposits, ws_residential_mortgages, ws_commercial_loans, ws_consumer_loans, ws_trading_assets, ws_market_risk_factor, ws_gross_income, ws_operational_factor)
    ws_capital_action, plan_gap_amount = capital_planning_C14(ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_growth_rate, ws_target_ratio, ws_total_capital, ws_retained_earnings_proj, ws_sub_debt_capacity, ws_plan_update_date, capital_plan_record, ws_capital_plan)
    ws_stress_pass_fail = stress_testing_C14(ws_scenario_name, ws_rate_shock, ws_gdp_change, ws_unemployment_rate, ws_housing_decline, ws_loan_portfolio, ws_stress_lgd, ws_stress_pd, ws_total_capital, ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_min_capital_ratio, ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject)
    
    return ws_tier1_capital, ws_tier2_capital, ws_total_capital, ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_capital_action, plan_gap_amount, ws_stress_pass_fail

#34100-calculate_capital_ratios
#            PERFORM 34110-calculate_tier1
#            PERFORM 34120-calculate_tier2
#            PERFORM 34130-calculate_ratios.
def calculate_capital_ratios_C14(ws_common_stock: Decimal, ws_retained_earnings: Decimal, ws_aoci: Decimal, ws_goodwill: Decimal, ws_intangibles: Decimal, ws_dta_deduction: Decimal, ws_sub_debt: Decimal, ws_alll_eligible: Decimal, ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal, ws_total_assets: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_tier1_capital = calculate_tier1_C14(ws_common_stock, ws_retained_earnings, ws_aoci, ws_goodwill, ws_intangibles, ws_dta_deduction)
    ws_tier2_capital, ws_total_capital = calculate_tier2_C14(ws_sub_debt, ws_alll_eligible, ws_tier1_capital)
    ws_cet1_ratio, ws_capital_ratio, ws_leverage_ratio = calculate_ratios_C14(ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_total_assets, ws_tier1_capital, ws_total_capital)
    
    return ws_tier1_capital, ws_tier2_capital, ws_total_capital, ws_cet1_ratio, ws_capital_ratio, ws_leverage_ratio

#34110-calculate_tier1
#            MOVE ZEROES TO ws_tier1_capital
#            ADD ws_common_stock TO ws_tier1_capital
#            ADD ws_retained_earnings TO ws_tier1_capital
#            ADD ws_aoci TO ws_tier1_capital
#            SUBTRACT ws_goodwill FROM ws_tier1_capital
#            SUBTRACT ws_intangibles FROM ws_tier1_capital
#            SUBTRACT ws_dta_deduction FROM ws_tier1_capital.
def calculate_tier1_C14(ws_common_stock: Decimal, ws_retained_earnings: Decimal, ws_aoci: Decimal, ws_goodwill: Decimal, ws_intangibles: Decimal, ws_dta_deduction: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_tier1_capital = Decimal(0)
    ws_tier1_capital += ws_common_stock
    ws_tier1_capital += ws_retained_earnings
    ws_tier1_capital += ws_aoci
    ws_tier1_capital -= ws_goodwill
    ws_tier1_capital -= ws_intangibles
    ws_tier1_capital -= ws_dta_deduction
    return ws_tier1_capital

#34120-calculate_tier2
#            MOVE ZEROES TO ws_tier2_capital
#            ADD ws_sub_debt TO ws_tier2_capital
#            ADD ws_alll_eligible TO ws_tier2_capital
#            COMPUTE ws_total_capital = 
#               ws_tier1_capital + ws_tier2_capital.
def calculate_tier2_C14(ws_sub_debt: Decimal, ws_alll_eligible: Decimal, ws_tier1_capital: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal]:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_tier2_capital = Decimal(0)
    ws_tier2_capital += ws_sub_debt
    ws_tier2_capital += ws_alll_eligible
    ws_total_capital = ws_tier1_capital + ws_tier2_capital
    return ws_tier2_capital, ws_total_capital

#34130-calculate_ratios
#            IF ws_risk_weighted_assets > 0
#               COMPUTE ws_cet1_ratio = 
#                  (ws_tier1_capital / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100
#               COMPUTE ws_capital_ratio = 
#                  (ws_total_capital / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100
#            
#            IF ws_total_assets > 0
#               COMPUTE ws_leverage_ratio = 
#                  (ws_tier1_capital / ws_total_assets) * 100
#            
def calculate_ratios_C14(ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal, ws_total_assets: Decimal, ws_tier1_capital: Decimal, ws_total_capital: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """COBOL logic"""34200-risk_weighted_assets
               MOVE ZEROES TO ws_risk_weighted_assets
               PERFORM 34210-credit_rwa
               PERFORM 34220-market_rwa
               PERFORM 34230-operational_rwa."""COBOL logic"""34210-credit_rwa
               COMPUTE ws_cash_rwa = ws_cash_position * 0.00
               COMPUTE ws_govt_rwa = ws_govt_securities * 0.00
               COMPUTE ws_bank_rwa = ws_bank_deposits * 0.20
               COMPUTE ws_mortgage_rwa = ws_residential_mortgages * 0.50
               COMPUTE ws_commercial_rwa = ws_commercial_loans * 1.00
               COMPUTE ws_consumer_rwa = ws_consumer_loans * 1.00
               ADD ws_cash_rwa TO ws_risk_weighted_assets
               ADD ws_govt_rwa TO ws_risk_weighted_assets
               ADD ws_bank_rwa TO ws_risk_weighted_assets
               ADD ws_mortgage_rwa TO ws_risk_weighted_assets
               ADD ws_commercial_rwa TO ws_risk_weighted_assets
               ADD ws_consumer_rwa TO ws_risk_weighted_assets."""
    ws_cash_rwa = ws_cash_position * Decimal("0.00")
    ws_govt_rwa = ws_govt_securities * Decimal("0.00")
    ws_bank_rwa = ws_bank_deposits * Decimal("0.20")
    ws_mortgage_rwa = ws_residential_mortgages * Decimal("0.50")
    ws_commercial_rwa = ws_commercial_loans * Decimal("1.00")
    ws_consumer_rwa = ws_consumer_loans * Decimal("1.00")
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_cash_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_govt_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_bank_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_mortgage_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_commercial_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_consumer_rwa
    return ws_risk_weighted_assets

#34220-market_rwa
#            COMPUTE ws_market_rwa = 
#               ws_trading_assets * ws_market_risk_factor
#            ADD ws_market_rwa TO ws_risk_weighted_assets.
def market_rwa_C14(ws_trading_assets: Decimal, ws_market_risk_factor: Decimal, ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_market_rwa = ws_trading_assets * ws_market_risk_factor
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_market_rwa
    return ws_risk_weighted_assets

#34230-operational_rwa
#            COMPUTE ws_operational_rwa = 
#               ws_gross_income * ws_operational_factor * 12.5
#            ADD ws_operational_rwa TO ws_risk_weighted_assets.
def operational_rwa_C14(ws_gross_income: Decimal, ws_operational_factor: Decimal, ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_operational_rwa = ws_gross_income * ws_operational_factor * Decimal("12.5")
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_operational_rwa
    return ws_risk_weighted_assets

#34300-capital_planning
#            PERFORM 34310-project_capital_needs
#            PERFORM 34320-identify_capital_actions
#            PERFORM 34330-update_capital_plan.
def capital_planning_C14(ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal, ws_growth_rate: Decimal, ws_target_ratio: Decimal, ws_total_capital: Decimal, ws_retained_earnings_proj: Decimal, ws_sub_debt_capacity: Decimal, ws_plan_update_date: date, capital_plan_record: File, ws_capital_plan: "WSCapitalPlan") -> tuple[str, Decimal]:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_capital_gap, ws_required_capital, ws_projected_rwa = project_capital_needs_C14(ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_growth_rate, ws_target_ratio, ws_total_capital)
    ws_capital_action = identify_capital_actions_C14(ws_capital_gap, ws_retained_earnings_proj, ws_sub_debt_capacity)
    update_capital_plan_C14(ws_plan_update_date, capital_plan_record, ws_capital_plan, ws_capital_action, ws_capital_gap)
    
    return ws_capital_action, ws_capital_gap

#34310-project_capital_needs
#            COMPUTE ws_projected_rwa = 
#               ws_risk_weighted_assets * (1 + ws_growth_rate)
#            COMPUTE ws_required_capital = 
#               ws_projected_rwa * ws_target_ratio / 100
#            COMPUTE ws_capital_gap = 
#               ws_required_capital - ws_total_capital.
def project_capital_needs_C14(ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal, ws_growth_rate: Decimal, ws_target_ratio: Decimal, ws_total_capital: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_projected_rwa = ws_risk_weighted_assets * (1 + ws_growth_rate)
    ws_required_capital = ws_projected_rwa * ws_target_ratio / 100
    ws_capital_gap = ws_required_capital - ws_total_capital
    return ws_capital_gap, ws_required_capital, ws_projected_rwa

#34320-identify_capital_actions
#            IF ws_capital_gap > 0
#               EVALUATE TRUE
#                  WHEN ws_capital_gap <= ws_retained_earnings_proj
#                     MOVE 'ORGANIC GROWTH' TO ws_capital_action
#                  WHEN ws_capital_gap <= ws_sub_debt_capacity
#                     MOVE 'SUB DEBT ISSUANCE' TO ws_capital_action
#                  WHEN OTHER
#                     MOVE 'EQUITY RAISE' TO ws_capital_action
#               
#            ELSE
#               MOVE 'NO ACTION NEEDED' TO ws_capital_action
#            
def identify_capital_actions_C14(ws_capital_gap: Decimal, ws_retained_earnings_proj: Decimal, ws_sub_debt_capacity: Decimal) -> str:
    """34320-identify_capital_actions
               IF ws_capital_gap > 0
                  EVALUATE TRUE
                     WHEN ws_capital_gap <= ws_retained_earnings_proj
                        MOVE 'ORGANIC GROWTH' TO ws_capital_action
                     WHEN ws_capital_gap <= ws_sub_debt_capacity
                        MOVE 'SUB DEBT ISSUANCE' TO ws_capital_action
                     WHEN OTHER
                        MOVE 'EQUITY RAISE' TO ws_capital_action

                  MOVE 'NO ACTION NEEDED' TO ws_capital_action
               
    if ws_capital_gap > 0:
        if ws_capital_gap <= ws_retained_earnings_proj:
            ws_capital_action = 'ORGANIC GROWTH'
        elif ws_capital_gap <= ws_sub_debt_capacity:
            ws_capital_action = 'SUB DEBT ISSUANCE'
        else:
            ws_capital_action = 'EQUITY RAISE'
    else:
        ws_capital_action = 'NO ACTION NEEDED'
    return ws_capital_action

#34330-update_capital_plan
#            MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO ws_plan_update_date
#            MOVE ws_capital_action TO plan_recommended_action"""
#            MOVE ws_capital_gap TO plan_gap_amount"""
#            REWRITE capital_plan_record FROM ws_capital_plan."""

    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_plan_update_date = date.today()
    plan_recommended_action = ws_capital_action
    plan_gap_amount = ws_capital_gap
    #rewrite_capital_plan_record(capital_plan_record, ws_capital_plan)
    pass

#34400-stress_testing
#            PERFORM 34410-run_baseline
#            PERFORM 34420-run_adverse
#            PERFORM 34430-run_severely_adverse
#            PERFORM 34440-compile_results.
def stress_testing_C14(ws_scenario_name: str, ws_rate_shock: Decimal, ws_gdp_change: Decimal, ws_unemployment_rate: Decimal, ws_housing_decline: Decimal, ws_loan_portfolio: Decimal, ws_stress_lgd: Decimal, ws_stress_pd: Decimal, ws_total_capital: Decimal, ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal, ws_min_capital_ratio: Decimal, ws_notif_type: str, ws_notif_channel: str, ws_notif_subject: str) -> str:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_scenario_name = 'BASELINE'
    ws_rate_shock = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_gdp_change = Decimal("2.50")
    ws_unemployment_rate = Decimal("4.00")
    ws_housing_decline = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_stress_pass_fail = calculate_stress_impact_C14(ws_loan_portfolio, ws_stress_lgd, ws_stress_pd, ws_trading_assets, ws_rate_shock, ws_total_capital, ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_min_capital_ratio)
    ws_scenario_name = 'ADVERSE'
    ws_rate_shock = Decimal("2.00")
    ws_gdp_change = Decimal("-1.50")
    ws_unemployment_rate = Decimal("7.00")
    ws_housing_decline = Decimal("-15.00")
    ws_stress_pass_fail = calculate_stress_impact_C14(ws_loan_portfolio, ws_stress_lgd, ws_stress_pd, ws_trading_assets, ws_rate_shock, ws_total_capital, ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_min_capital_ratio)
    ws_scenario_name = 'severely_adverse'
    ws_rate_shock = Decimal("3.00")
    ws_gdp_change = Decimal("-6.00")
    ws_unemployment_rate = Decimal("10.00")
    ws_housing_decline = Decimal("-30.00")
    ws_stress_pass_fail = calculate_stress_impact_C14(ws_loan_portfolio, ws_stress_lgd, ws_stress_pd, ws_trading_assets, ws_rate_shock, ws_total_capital, ws_risk_weighted_assets, ws_min_capital_ratio)
    
    if ws_stress_pass_fail == 'FAIL':
        remediation_actions_C14(ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject)
    return ws_stress_pass_fail

#34410-run_baseline

    return ws_stress_pass_fail

def load_ic_balances_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_IC_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_IC_ARRAY, INTERCOMPANY_FILE

    WS_IC_COUNT = 0
    # Simulate reading from file, populate WS_IC_ARRAY
    # Replace this with actual file reading logic
    
    # Example: Reading from a list of dictionaries
    ic_data = [
        {"ic_from_entity": "A", "ic_to_entity": "B", "ic_amount": Decimal("100.00")},
        {"ic_from_entity": "B", "ic_to_entity": "A", "ic_amount": Decimal("-100.00")},
        {"ic_from_entity": "C", "ic_to_entity": "D", "ic_amount": Decimal("50.00")},
    ]
    
    for item in ic_data:
        if WS_EOF_FLAG == 'Y':
            break
        
        WS_IC_COUNT += 1
        
        # Create a WsIcBalance instance and populate its fields
        ws_ic_balance = WsIcBalance(
            ic_from_entity=item["ic_from_entity"],
            ic_to_entity=item["ic_to_entity"],
            ic_amount=item["ic_amount"]
        )
        
        if WS_IC_COUNT <= len(WS_IC_ARRAY):
            WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_COUNT - 1] = ws_ic_balance
        else:
            print("WS_IC_ARRAY is full")
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    
    if not ic_data:
        WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def match_ic_pairs_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""COBOL: 37325-find_ic_counterpart
       MOVE ic_from_entity(ws_ic_idx) TO ws_search_from
       MOVE ic_to_entity(ws_ic_idx) TO ws_search_to
       PERFORM VARYING ws_ic_idx2 FROM 1 BY 1
          UNTIL ws_ic_idx2 > ws_ic_count
          IF ic_from_entity(ws_ic_idx2) = ws_search_to
             IF ic_to_entity(ws_ic_idx2) = ws_search_from
                COMPUTE ws_ic_diff = 
                   ic_amount(ws_ic_idx) + 0  # TODO
                   ic_amount(ws_ic_idx2)
                IF ws_ic_diff NOT  = None  # TODO: was ZEROES
                   PERFORM 37326-log_ic_diff
                
                EXIT PERFORM
             
          
       
    global WS_IC_IDX, WS_SEARCH_FROM, WS_SEARCH_TO, WS_IC_IDX2, WS_IC_COUNT, WS_IC_DIFF, WS_IC_ARRAY
    if WS_IC_IDX <= len(WS_IC_ARRAY) and WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX - 1] is not None:
        WS_SEARCH_FROM = WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX - 1].ic_from_entity
        WS_SEARCH_TO = WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX - 1].ic_to_entity
    else:
        return # Exit if WS_IC_IDX is out of range
    
    WS_IC_IDX2 = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX2 <= WS_IC_COUNT:
        if WS_IC_IDX2 <= len(WS_IC_ARRAY) and WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1] is not None:
            if WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1].ic_from_entity == WS_SEARCH_TO:
                if WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1].ic_to_entity == WS_SEARCH_FROM:
                    if WS_IC_IDX <= len(WS_IC_ARRAY) and WS_IC_IDX2 <= len(WS_IC_ARRAY) and WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX - 1] is not None and WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1] is not None:
                        WS_IC_DIFF = WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX - 1].ic_amount + WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1].ic_amount
                        if WS_IC_DIFF != Decimal("0.00"):
                            log_ic_diff_C15()
                        break  # EXIT PERFORM
        WS_IC_IDX2 += 1

from datetime import datetime

class WsAuditRecord:  # Define a class to represent the COBOL record structure.  Needs proper attributes later.
    def __init__(self, ws_audit_timestamp=None):
        self.ws_audit_key = None
        self.ws_audit_old_value = None
        self.ws_audit_new_value = None
        self.ws_audit_timestamp = ws_audit_timestamp
        self.ws_audit_user = None
        self.ws_audit_action = None
    def __str__(self):
        return f"WsAuditRecord(timestamp={self.ws_audit_timestamp}, user={self.ws_audit_user}, action={self.ws_audit_action})"

def archive_audit_logs_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_AUDIT_RECORD, WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP, WS_ARCHIVE_DATE, AUDIT_FILE, ARCHIVE_AUDIT_RECORD

    # Simulate reading audit logs from file
    audit_log_data = [
        {"ws_audit_timestamp": "20240101"},
        {"ws_audit_timestamp": "20231201"},
        {"ws_audit_timestamp": "20240201"}
    ]  # Replace with actual data source

    audit_log_index = 0

    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        if audit_log_index >= len(audit_log_data):
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
            break

        WS_AUDIT_RECORD = WsAuditRecord(**audit_log_data[audit_log_index])

        if WS_AUDIT_RECORD.ws_audit_timestamp < WS_ARCHIVE_DATE:
            ARCHIVE_AUDIT_RECORD = str(WS_AUDIT_RECORD)  # Simulate archiving
            # DELETE audit_file   #simulate delete

        audit_log_index += 1

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def cpu_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL: 39110-cpu_metrics
       CALL 'GETCPU' USING ws_cpu_utilization
       IF ws_cpu_utilization > 80
          MOVE 'Y' TO ws_cpu_alert

    global WS_CPU_UTILIZATION, WS_CPU_ALERT
    WS_CPU_UTILIZATION = getcpu_C15()  # Simulate CALL 'GETCPU'"""
    global WS_CPU_UTILIZATION, WS_CPU_ALERT

    WS_CPU_UTILIZATION = random.randint(0, 100)  # Simulate CPU utilization

    if WS_CPU_UTILIZATION > 80:
        WS_CPU_ALERT = 'Y'
